/usr/lib/gcc/x86_64-linux-gnu/14/32/libgcc.a
{ echo "/* made by the Makefile: the kernels this loader starts under Secure Boot */"; \
  echo "static const u8 KERNEL32_SHA256[32] = { $(sha256sum kernel.bin | cut -c1-64 | sed 's/../0x&,/g') };"; \
  echo "static const u8 KERNEL64_SHA256[32] = { $(sha256sum kernel64.bin | cut -c1-64 | sed 's/../0x&,/g') };"; } > loader/kernel_hashes.h
gcc -m64 -mno-red-zone -O2 -ffreestanding -fpie -fno-stack-protector -fno-stack-check -fshort-wchar -mgeneral-regs-only -fno-asynchronous-unwind-tables -fvisibility=hidden -Wall -Wextra -c loader/efi.c -o loader/efi64.o
gcc -m32 -malign-double -O2 -ffreestanding -fpie -fno-stack-protector -fno-stack-check -fshort-wchar -mgeneral-regs-only -fno-asynchronous-unwind-tables -fvisibility=hidden -Wall -Wextra -c loader/efi.c -o loader/efi32.o
gcc -m32 -c loader/tramp32.S -o loader/tramp32.o
ld -m elf_i386 -nostdlib -pie --no-dynamic-linker -z nocombreloc -T loader/efi32.lds -o loader/bootia32.so loader/efi32.o loader/tramp32.o
python3 tools/efi_relocs.py loader/bootia32.so
gcc -m64 -c loader/tramp.S -o loader/tramp64.o
objcopy -j .text -j .reloc -j .got -j .data -j .dynamic -j .rela -j .rel -j .dynsym -O efi-app-ia32 --subsystem=10 loader/bootia32.so loader/BOOTIA32.EFI.unsigned
ld -m elf_x86_64 -nostdlib -pie --no-dynamic-linker -z nocombreloc -T loader/efi.lds -o loader/bootx64.so loader/efi64.o loader/tramp64.o
note: no Secure Boot key at /var/lib/yhello/.banana-secureboot/banana-sb.key - loader/BOOTIA32.EFI is not signed
python3 tools/efi_relocs.py loader/bootx64.so
objcopy -j .text -j .reloc -j .got -j .data -j .dynamic -j .rela -j .rel -j .dynsym -O efi-app-x86_64 --subsystem=10 loader/bootx64.so loader/BOOTX64.EFI.unsigned
note: no Secure Boot key at /var/lib/yhello/.banana-secureboot/banana-sb.key - loader/BOOTX64.EFI is not signed
mkdir -p isoroot
rm -f isoroot/efi.img
printf 'set banana_medium=live-cd\nset banana_video=auto        \n' > isoroot/medium.cfg
kb=$(( ($(stat -c %s kernel.bin) + $(stat -c %s kernel64.bin) + 2 * $(stat -c %s loader/BOOTX64.EFI)) / 1024 + 2048 )); \
    dd if=/dev/zero of=isoroot/efi.img bs=1024 count=$kb status=none
mformat -i isoroot/efi.img -v BANANA_EFI ::
mmd -i isoroot/efi.img ::/EFI ::/EFI/BOOT ::/EFI/BananaOS ::/boot
mcopy -i isoroot/efi.img loader/BOOTX64.EFI ::/EFI/BOOT/BOOTX64.EFI
mcopy -i isoroot/efi.img loader/BOOTIA32.EFI ::/EFI/BOOT/BOOTIA32.EFI
mcopy -i isoroot/efi.img isoroot/medium.cfg loader/keys/BananaOS.cer ::/EFI/BananaOS/
if [ -f loader/keys/PK.auth ]; then mcopy -i isoroot/efi.img loader/keys/PK.auth loader/keys/KEK.auth loader/keys/db.auth ::/EFI/BananaOS/; fi
mcopy -i isoroot/efi.img kernel.bin kernel64.bin ::/boot/
mkdir -p isoroot/boot
cp kernel.bin kernel64.bin loader/bios.bin isoroot/boot/
xorriso -as mkisofs -quiet -o Banana_OS.iso -R -J -V BANANA_OS \
    -b boot/bios.bin -no-emul-boot -boot-load-size 4 -boot-info-table \
    -eltorito-alt-boot -e efi.img -no-emul-boot isoroot
